- A single-line free-text `role` input, labelled "Your role (e.g. researcher, field documenter)" with placeholder "Researcher, field documenter, reviewer...". This is a typed self-description, NOT the account's UserRole.
  - `frontend/app/(protected)/feedback/page.tsx:296-305`
- Validation on the web is one rule only: `anyProvided` — at least one rating truthy or one text non-blank. Otherwise submit sets the error "Add at least one rating or a written answer first." The submit button is also disabled while !anyProvided.
  - `frontend/app/(protected)/feedback/page.tsx:226-235,324`
- Submit path: PUT /feedback/me with the whole ratings object plus the texts, each trimmed and coerced to null when empty ("A cleared box means 'no answer', which the column stores as NULL, not as ''"). Success message: "Thank you — your feedback has been saved. You can revisit and change it any time."
  - `frontend/app/(protected)/feedback/page.tsx:240-249`
- On mount the web page GETs /feedback/me and prefills every field; the button label switches between "Submit feedback" and "Update feedback", with "Last updated <datetime>" beside it.
  - `frontend/app/(protected)/feedback/page.tsx:182-207,325-327`
- Backend router: prefix /feedback, tags ["feedback"], three endpoints — GET /feedback/me, PUT /feedback/me, GET /feedback.
  - `backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:12,84,91,108`
- The router is registered on the main API router.
  - `backend/app/api/router.py:125`
- GET /feedback/me returns the caller's own row via find_unique on userId, or `{}` when none exists.
  - `backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:84-88`
- PUT /feedback/me is an UPSERT keyed on userId — it writes ALL 13 FEEDBACK_FIELDS on both create and update, so any field absent from the payload is written as NULL and the previous answer is destroyed.
  - `backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:97-104`
- FEEDBACK_FIELDS is the single tuple keeping the serializer and the upsert in lockstep: rating, easeOfUse, reliability, performance, design, features, recommend, comment, likeMost, improve, bugs, featureRequests, role.
  - `backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:42-56`
- GET /feedback is gated by require_master_admin — MASTER_ADMIN only, 403 "Master admin access required" otherwise.
  - `backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:111; backend/app/core/deps.py:698-704`
- GET /feedback returns a BARE JSON ARRAY (not the page_payload envelope every sibling list route uses) because the web page does apiFetch<Feedback[]>("/feedback") and maps it; the docstring records this as a deliberate constraint pending a two-sided envelope migration.
  - `backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:114-124`
- GET /feedback is bounded at FEEDBACK_TAKE = 200 rows, ordered [{updatedAt desc}, {id desc}] with the id as a load-bearing tiebreaker, paged by page/pageSize, and reports X-Total-Count and X-Truncated headers; a truncated parameterless read is also logged at ERROR.
  - `backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:37,113,159-187`
- _serialize attaches only id/name/email/role from the joined author, explicitly never the password hash or other sensitive user columns.
  - `backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:59-81`
- FeedbackUpsertRequest validation: seven ints each Field(default=None, ge=1, le=5); comment/likeMost/improve/bugs/featureRequests each max_length=5000; role max_length=200. Every field individually optional — the schema does NOT enforce "at least one".
  - `backend/app/schemas/feedback.py:6-25`
- There is no feedback service module — `backend/app/services/` contains no feedback file; all logic lives in the route.
  - `backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:1-11`
- Prisma model Feedback: id (cuid), userId (@unique), seven nullable Ints, six nullable Strings (comment, likeMost, improve, bugs, featureRequests, role), createdAt @default(now()), updatedAt @updatedAt, a User relation "FeedbackUser" with onDelete: Cascade, and @@index([updatedAt]). That is the ENTIRE table — 15 columns.
  - `backend/prisma/schema.prisma:349-373`
- Two migrations built the table: 20260619120000_feedback created it with only rating + comment; 20260619170000_feedback_detail added the six sub-ratings, the four extra text prompts and role.
  - `backend/prisma/migrations/20260619120000_feedback/migration.sql:1-19; backend/prisma/migrations/20260619170000_feedback_detail/migration.sql:1-12`
- Web admin view: the "All feedback" section renders only for isMasterAdmin(user), and the GET /feedback fetch is skipped entirely for anyone else. Each card shows the author's name, email, updatedAt, and a SavedFeedbackDetail summary.

Anything unlisted is open to any signed-in user, and the comment names feedback among those.

- A SEPARATE, far more research-shaped rating instrument already exists in this codebase for design-workshop peer review — DwReviewRating carries designWorkshopId, stageEntryId, entityKey, reviewerId, round (DwReviewRound), a CHECK-constrained 1-5 score, split comment/suggestion columns, and a device-clock `recordedAt` distinct from server `createdAt`. It is the local precedent for what contextualised, linked, round-based feedback looks like here.

### Gaps — what the requirement asks for that is not there

- **NO TASK CONTEXT. Nothing records what the user was doing, which record/workshop they were on, or which task they had just completed. The instrument is a global app-satisfaction survey answered out of band.**
  - evidence: The Feedback table's complete column list is id, userId, 7 ints, 6 strings, createdAt, updatedAt — no context column of any kind (backend/prisma/schema.prisma:349-373). FEEDBACK_FIELDS confirms the wire contract carries nothing else (backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:42-56). The only place task context could arrive is the free-text `bugs` placeholder asking the user to type it themselves: "What you were doing, what you expected, and what happened instead." (frontend/app/(protected)/feedback/page.tsx:72).
  - change here: backend/prisma/schema.prisma model Feedback; a new migration under backend/prisma/migrations/; FEEDBACK_FIELDS in backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:42; FeedbackUpsertRequest in backend/app/schemas/feedback.py; the `Feedback` type and the submit body in frontend/app/(protected)/feedback/page.tsx:19-38,240-247; FeedbackDto + FeedbackUpsertRequest in android/.../data/ApiModels.kt:374-412.
  - android twin: android/app/src/main/java/com/designprototype/workshop/MainActivity.kt:10824 FeedbackScreen (same absence — it collects the same 13 fields and nothing more)
- **NO TARGET FEATURE OR SCREEN. Feedback cannot be attributed to a feature. The six aspects (ease of use, reliability, performance, design, features, recommend) are whole-app attributes; there is no field naming the module being rated, so 'the questionnaire flow is slow' and 'sketch upload is slow' both land as one `performance: 2`.**
  - evidence: RATING_FIELDS is a hard-coded 6-entry const of global app aspects (frontend/app/(protected)/feedback/page.tsx:41-48), mirrored as six fixed Int columns (backend/prisma/schema.prisma:353-359) and six fixed LabeledStarRating calls (android/.../MainActivity.kt:10898-10903). No column, prop or DTO field names a feature.

### Traps and risks

- THE BARE-ARRAY CONTRACT IS LOAD-BEARING AND TESTED. GET /feedback must keep answering a bare JSON array — the web page does apiFetch<Feedback[]>("/feedback").then(setAllFeedback) and maps it, so an items/total/pages envelope replaces the master admin's screen with a runtime error on deploy. This is documented at backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:117-124 and pinned by backend/tests/test_feedback_paging.py:12-16. Any envelope migration must land on both sides at once.
- THE 13-FIELD CONTRACT IS DUPLICATED IN SIX PLACES. Adding a column means editing ALL of: backend/prisma/schema.prisma:349, a new migration, FEEDBACK_FIELDS at backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:42, FeedbackUpsertRequest at backend/app/schemas/feedback.py:6, the Feedback type + state objects at frontend/app/(protected)/feedback/page.tsx:19-38,158-174, and BOTH Kotlin DTOs at android/.../ApiModels.kt:375,398. Missing any one of them silently drops the field on that surface.
- PUT /feedback/me OVERWRITES THE WHOLE ROW. backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:97 builds `fields` from every FEEDBACK_FIELDS entry unconditionally, so a client that has not been updated to send a new field will write it as NULL and erase what the other client saved. This makes staged rollout across web and Android dangerous: ship the column, then both clients, before anyone submits.
- ANDROID IS THE WORDING SOURCE OF TRUTH AND THE PARITY IS DELIBERATE. frontend/app/(protected)/feedback/page.tsx:40 ("in the Android screen's order and with its exact wording"), :51-55 (placeholders explicitly web-only, and why) and :89 (the OVERALL_WORDS ladder is "Android parity (FeedbackScreen)"). Changing a label on one platform alone breaks a stated invariant; the placeholders are the ONE documented intentional divergence.
- X-Total-Count / X-Truncated ARE UNREADABLE CROSS-ORIGIN. create_app builds CORSMiddleware with allow_headers=["*"] and NO expose_headers, and allow_headers governs the request direction only — a cross-origin deployment cannot read either header until expose_headers is added in backend/app/main.py. Documented at backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:139-149. The ERROR log at :182 is the only signal that survives.
- DO NOT REMOVE THE COUNT QUERY IN GET /feedback. backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:152-158 explicitly warns that inferring truncation from `len(rows) == pageSize` reports a false shortfall on an exactly-full final page. The count and the page are issued together through gather_reads for latency; the exactness is the point.
- THE `id` TIEBREAKER IN THE ORDER IS LOAD-BEARING. order=[{updatedAt desc}, {id desc}] at backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:166 — updatedAt is not unique on an upsert-per-account table, and a non-total order under LIMIT/OFFSET lets a row appear on two pages and another on none.
- DROPPING `userId @unique` TO GET LONGITUDINAL DATA BREAKS THREE READS AT ONCE: find_unique in my_feedback (backend/app/api/routes/feedback.py:87), the upsert's where clause (:99), and Android's MasterFeedbackCard which keys its dropdown and lookup on userId assuming one row per user (android/.../MainActivity.kt:11029-11036 `feedback.firstOrNull { it.userId == selectedUserId }`).
- FEEDBACK IS DELETED WITH THE ACCOUNT. onDelete: Cascade at backend/prisma/schema.prisma:370 — removing a participant's account destroys their research data with no archive. For a study or a patent record this is a retention hazard that nothing currently mitigates.
- THE ROUTE IS INTENTIONALLY UNGUARDED ON THE WEB. /feedback is absent from ROUTE_GUARDS by design (frontend/lib/permissions.ts:306-308 names feedback among the correct open defaults). Adding a guard would lock ordinary users out of giving feedback — the gate belongs on the admin LIST section (page.tsx:339), which is where it already is.
- THERE IS NO SERVICE LAYER FOR FEEDBACK. backend/app/services/ contains no feedback module; all logic is inline in the route. Any non-trivial addition (context capture, aggregation, export) has no existing home and should follow the pattern of services/design_workshop_viewers, which the route's own comments repeatedly cite as the precedent for bounded reads.
- ANDROID'S ADMIN CARD RENDERS THE WHOLE LIST WITH NO PAGING and computes its average over only the rows it received (android/.../MainActivity.kt:11017-11023) — with the 200-row cap in place that average is silently wrong past 200 accounts, and Android reads neither X-Total-Count nor X-Truncated (WorkshopRepositoryApi.kt:77-78 returns a plain List<FeedbackDto>).

## 7. Web sketch upload — Sketches & Prototypes → Upload tab (frontend/components/sketches/**)

**Summary.** There is exactly ONE `<input type="file">` implementation in the whole sketches tree — `upload/DropCard.tsx:236` — and it is instantiated three times on the Upload tab: once in the Sketches half (SketchTraceField.tsx:1790, "Choose a photograph") and twice in the Prototypes half (PrototypeModelField.tsx:344 turntable, :408 model file). "Measure a dimension from a photograph" has NO uploader of any kind: `MeasureFromPhotoCard` takes already-attached row photographs resolved to displayable URLs by `useMeasurablePhotos` (UploadTabHost.tsx:275-384), and `PhotoMeasureField` reads no bytes at all. So the premise "two cards each with their own uploader" is false on this surface — it is one uploader (buried two clicks deep behind a disclosure and behind the trace engine loading) plus one card that can only see what is already filed. The two cards are block siblings inside one `.panel p-4` with no grid or col-span between them, and they are visually inconsistent in three ways at once: collapsed, the trace card is an auto-width `field-button-secondary` inline button while the measure card is a full-width bordered `<section>`; open, the trace card is `rounded-lg … p-3` and the measure card is `rounded-md` with padding on its children instead; and their spacing above differs (`mt-2` self-applied vs `mt-3` applied by the parent). The measure card's subtitle is unclamped and spans the full card width; the trace card's equivalent line is `max-w-prose`-clamped AND only exists once the panel is opened — collapsed, the trace card has no description line at all.

### What exists today

- The ONLY `<input type="file">` in frontend/components/sketches/ is inside DropCard. Grep for `type="file"` across the whole sketches tree returns exactly one hit. Markup: `<input ref={inputRef} type="file" accept={accept} multiple={multiple} className="sr-only" tabIndex={-1} aria-hidden="true" disabled={disabled} onChange={…} />`
  - `F:/Portal_Development_Designer/frontend/components/sketches/upload/DropCard.tsx:236-263`
- DropCard's visible control is a real button carrying the lucide `Upload` icon: `<button type="button" className="field-button" disabled={disabled} aria-describedby={`${cardId}-accept`} onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()}><Upload className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />{buttonLabel}</button>`
  - `F:/Portal_Development_Designer/frontend/components/sketches/upload/DropCard.tsx:215-224`
- UPLOAD CONTROL #1 — the Sketches half's only one. `<DropCard label="Photograph to trace" buttonLabel="Choose a photograph" accept={TRACEABLE_ACCEPT} acceptSentence={…} disabled={disabled} validate={…} onFiles={(files) => { const chosen = files[0]; if (chosen) void chooseFile(chosen); }}>`, wrapped in `<div className="mb-3">` at :1789. TRACEABLE_ACCEPT is the literal string "image/*" (decodeToPixels.ts:80).
  - `F:/Portal_Development_Designer/frontend/components/sketches/upload/SketchTraceField.tsx:1789-1830`
- That DropCard is gated TWICE: it only renders when `phase.status === "ready"` (the trace engine has finished loading) and only inside `panel`, which only renders when `open` is true. `const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)` — the tab opens with the panel CLOSED.
  - `F:/Portal_Development_Designer/frontend/components/sketches/upload/SketchTraceField.tsx:347, 1773, 2573`

  - `F:/Portal_Development_Designer/frontend/components/sketches/upload/MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx:117-132, 145`

The panel it wraps deliberately reads no pixels, which is why a URL suffices: "`PhotoMeasureField` reads no pixels — its own header says so under 'NO NETWORK, NO CANVAS READBACK, NO RE-ENCODING' … So this needs a URL and nothing else."

### Gaps — what the requirement asks for that is not there

- **There is no single/unified upload control on the Upload tab. The Sketches half has exactly ONE file picker and it is two clicks and one async engine load away; the Prototypes half has two more with different `accept` and multiplicity; and the measure card has none. Nothing on this surface accepts a photograph once and serves both cards.**
  - evidence: Only file input in the tree is DropCard.tsx:236, instantiated at SketchTraceField.tsx:1790, PrototypeModelField.tsx:344 and :408. `MeasureFromPhotoCard` takes only `photos: MeasurePhotos` (MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx:145) whose members are `{key,name,url}` (PhotoMeasureField.tsx:78).
  - change here: F:/Portal_Development_Designer/frontend/components/sketches/upload/UploadTabPanel.tsx (the composition point — the sketch branch at :210-233 is where a shared picker would sit above both children); a new prop pair on SketchTraceField.tsx (`SketchTraceFieldProps` at :243-263) to let a File be pushed IN rather than only picked inside; a new optional prop on MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx (`MeasureFromPhotoCardProps` at :134-161) to accept a not-yet-attached photo; and UploadTabHost.tsx:1117-1124 / :992-999 which supply both today.
  - android twin: DwSketchTraceCropPanel.kt and DwPhotoMeasureField.kt (cited from web source at SketchTraceField.tsx:1838, MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx:28-29 and :273) — not read in this pass; the handset is documented as mounting DwPhotoMeasurePanel from both the stage form (FieldRenderer.kt:1853) and ui/RecordMeasureField.kt:408.
- **A unified uploader must produce TWO different artefacts from one pick, and neither card can consume the other's. The trace side needs the raw `File` (for `chooseFile` and for `onAttachSource`, which hands the untouched File to the host) plus the `DecodedPixels` it derives. The measure side needs only a displayable URL string, a stable `key` and a `name` — it never touches bytes. So the unifier owns `URL.createObjectURL(file)` and its revoke, which today lives only inside the host's effect.**
  - evidence: `chooseFile(chosen: File)` → `decodeToPixels(chosen)` (SketchTraceField.tsx:762-792); `onAttachSource(chosen)` takes a File (SketchTraceField.tsx:1523, type at :262). `MeasurablePhoto = { key; name; url }` (PhotoMeasureField.tsx:78). URL create/revoke pairing only at UploadTabHost.tsx:332-333, 357, 375.
  - change here: Whoever owns the new shared state must own the object-URL lifecycle in the same effect (the rule is spelled out at UploadTabHost.tsx:268-273). Most natural home is UploadTabHost.tsx alongside `useMeasurablePhotos` (:275-384), since that is already the only place that knows how to turn a reference into a URL.
  - android twin: NONE verified in this pass.
- **The measure card currently CANNOT show a freshly-picked photograph — only ones already written into the row. A unified upload changes its contract: today the only route from picking to measuring is attach → putDraftStage → reload → re-resolve from IndexedDB, so an unattached photo is invisible to it and a photo attached to a DIFFERENT row than the one selected is also invisible.**
  - evidence: `sketchPhotoRefs` is derived purely from `chosenSketch?.[field.key]` (UploadTabHost.tsx:762-765); `attach` calls `await reload()` at :529 and again at :557; the empty branch prints "No photograph has been attached to “{rowName}” yet…" (MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx:376-385).
  - change here: MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx `MeasurePhotos` union (:117-132) and the `Body` branch order (:304-386) — a seventh state "a photograph is chosen but not yet filed" has to be inserted without breaking the documented branch ordering at :296-302.

### Traps and risks

- e2e/sketch-trace-panel.spec.ts pins the collapsed disclosure hard: `getByRole("button", { name: "Trace a sketch into line art" })` appears 27 times and `getByRole("button", { name: "Choose a photograph" })` 5 times. It also asserts the SAME string as a role=heading after opening (:622) and asserts that heading is FOCUSED (:626), and asserts the trigger has count 0 while open (:653). Replacing the trigger with an always-visible unified uploader breaks all of these; the crop-readout string is separately pinned at :1348 (cited from SketchTraceField.tsx:1851).
- e2e/sketches-persistence-unit.spec.ts reads UploadTabPanel.tsx and PrototypeModelField.tsx as raw SOURCE TEXT and asserts substrings against them (:43-48, :230, :250-252, :261, :274-279, :300). Editing those files' wording or JSX can fail CI with no behavioural change at all — e.g. `expect(MODEL_SOURCE.match(/if \(handed === false\) return;/g)).toHaveLength(2)` at :252.
- "Measure a dimension from a photograph" is hardcoded in FOUR places across two clients and is deliberately NOT imported from a shared symbol: MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx:106, PhotoMeasureField.tsx:539 and :567, and android DwPhotoMeasureField.kt:371 and :612 (per the note at MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx:94-105). A retitle is a multi-file, cross-client edit.
- `sourceFiledRef` guarantees `onAttachSource` is called AT MOST ONCE per chosen File and is reset only inside `chooseFile` (SketchTraceField.tsx:1512-1524, :783). A unified picker that sets `file` by any other route re-arms the duplicate-upload bug that ref exists to prevent.
- `pickRef` (SketchTraceField.tsx:762-764, :785) is the guard against A-then-B pick races; its header at :755-761 records the exact symptom ("the panel then traces A, attaches the drawing under B's name"). Moving the pick outside this component without carrying the token re-arms it.
- UploadTabPanel is a SHARED unit with a second host — a record form that deliberately omits `onAttachSketchSource` and `onAttachTurntable` because it has its own image field (UploadTabPanel.tsx:20-26, :98, :104-109). A unified uploader added unconditionally to the sketch branch would give that host a second, duplicate picker for the same bytes.
- The two cards live in MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE branches (`section === "sketch"` vs `"prototype"`, UploadTabPanel.tsx:210/234). Any "one upload for the tab" has to decide what the Prototypes side does, where there are two more pickers with different `accept` (`image/*` multiple, vs MODEL_ACCEPT single) and where `chooseFrames` — not `validate` — is deliberately the refusal rule (PrototypeModelField.tsx:333-343).
- Object-URL lifecycle is stated as load-bearing: created and revoked in one effect "because revoked anywhere else they either leak for the life of the tab or are released while an `<img>` is still reading them, and the second looks like a photograph that vanished" (UploadTabHost.tsx:268-273). A URL minted by a new unified uploader must be revoked by the same owner.
- Tint layering is deliberate: `.panel` is `bg-card`, the trace panel and the measure card are `bg-surface-50`, and PrototypeModelField's inner cards are `bg-card` (white on white). MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx:203-208 explicitly refuses `.panel` for its root ("a second white card-on-card is the visual noise the tab was already carrying too much of"). Normalising sizes must not silently normalise tints.
- MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx:70-80 explicitly refuses components/ui/Accordion.tsx for two named reasons (no foot control possible; its `mb-5`/`p-4` is "the vertical air this card was explicitly asked not to have"), and :246-252 states the padding scheme is intentional. A size fix that reaches for the shared Accordion overturns both.
- `PhotoMeasureField` is CONTROLLED from this card via `open` (literal true) and `onOpenChange` — landed for this one call site (MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx:425-449). Changing the card's disclosure shape can re-stack the two disclosures and reintroduce the double-heading defect described at :58-68.
- The trace card's collapsed state doubles as the panel's keyboard return target — `collapse()` focuses `triggerRef` (MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx:193-196) and the trace panel focuses its `tabIndex={-1}` `<h4>` on open (SketchTraceField.tsx:1744-1747). Removing either element drops focus to `<body>`.
- The card titles in the requirement brief are title-cased; the code is sentence-cased and the title-cased forms exist nowhere in the repo. Do not "restore" a string that was never there — the repo's own house rule (SketchTabs.tsx:33-38) records a prior instance of documentation describing UI that did not exist.

## 8. Web prototypes parity — the PROTOTYPES side of Sketches & Prototypes (Upload tab)

**Summary.** "Prototypes" is not a tab on the web. The screen has exactly two tabs, Upload and Review (frontend/components/sketches/SketchesWorkspace.tsx:129-142); inside Upload, `UploadTabPanel` draws a two-button section toggle — Sketches / Prototypes — and shows one section at a time (frontend/components/sketches/upload/UploadTabPanel.tsx:174, 181-208). The Prototypes section is a `.panel p-4` holding an h3, one `max-w-prose` subtitle, `PrototypeModelField` (two cards: the 360° turntable and the 3D model file), and a host-built "Measure a dimension from a photograph" card in a slot. There is NO prototype equivalent of the sketch trace/rectify experience and that is enforced in code, not merely absent: `offersSketchRectify` needs a FILE field whose folded key+label matches `/(lineart|linedrawing|vector|plate|artwork)/`, and the prototype entity's only FILE fields are `measurementSheet` and `modelFile`. The measure experience, by contrast, is already at full parity — the same `MeasureFromPhotoCard` is mounted for both halves with `what="prototype"`, and the prototype entity really does declare cm-unit length targets. The layout inconsistencies named in the brief DO exist on the prototypes side: two separate DropCard uploaders for two fields of one entity (Android already renders those same two field keys through one component in a loop), an asymmetric `flex-1`/`mt-2` vs `mt-3` treatment between the two cards, a third card in the same section using a different radius and background, and `PrototypeModelField.tsx` being the only file in `components/sketches/upload/` with no `max-w-prose` anywhere in it.

### What exists today

- There is no "Prototypes" TAB. The workspace declares exactly two tabs — Upload and Review. "Prototypes" appears as a label only in the REVIEW tab's entity switcher (`ENTITIES`, prototype first).
  - `frontend/components/sketches/SketchesWorkspace.tsx:129-147`
- The Prototypes SECTION is a client-side toggle inside the Upload tab: `const [section, setSection] = useState<Section>("sketch")` and two real `<button>`s with `aria-pressed`, labelled "Sketches" (PencilRuler icon) and "Prototypes" (Layers icon), in a `flex flex-wrap gap-2` strip.
  - `frontend/components/sketches/upload/UploadTabPanel.tsx:174, 181-208`
- Prototypes section container and heading: `<div className="panel p-4">` then `<h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-ink-900">Prototypes</h3>`. `.panel` resolves to `rounded-lg border border-line-200 bg-card shadow-sm`.
  - `frontend/components/sketches/upload/UploadTabPanel.tsx:235-236`
  - also: app/globals.css:327-329
- Prototypes section subtitle, verbatim: `<p className="mt-1 max-w-prose text-sm leading-6 text-ink-500">` — "A prototype reaches a reviewer two ways, and they are not equal. Photographs of it turning are placed in the ministry document as pictures; a 3D model file is listed there only as a count. Both are worth attaching — the panel below says which does what."
  - `frontend/components/sketches/upload/UploadTabPanel.tsx:237-241`
- The section body is `<div className="mt-3"><PrototypeModelField modelLabel turntableLabel turntableCount disabled onAttachModel onAttachTurntable /></div>`, followed by `{prototypeMeasure ? <div className="mt-3">{prototypeMeasure}</div> : null}`.
  - `frontend/components/sketches/upload/UploadTabPanel.tsx:242-253`
- `PrototypeModelField` root is `<div className="grid gap-3">` holding exactly two cards, turntable first (a comment says "The turntable comes first, because it is the half that prints").

If the actual wording of 5, 6 or 8 differs, re-read this section against it.**

- **APPLIES (unify uploaders). Prototypes has TWO separate uploaders where Sketches has one, and they are for two fields of the same entity. Unifying them is not just possible — it is already Android's shape, one component looped over `listOf("turntablePhotos", "modelFile")`.**
  - evidence: frontend/components/sketches/upload/PrototypeModelField.tsx:344 and :408 are two `<DropCard>`s; android/.../DwSketchChooserRows.kt:83 plus DwSketchChooserUpload.kt:706-726 render the same two keys through one `DwMediaCaptureCard`.

Callbacks stay in frontend/components/sketches/upload/UploadTabPanel.tsx:99-115 and their wiring in frontend/components/sketches/UploadTabHost.tsx:1043-1101.

- **BLOCKER ON THAT UNIFICATION: the two uploaders are NOT behaviourally the same, so a naive merge changes what a designer has to press. The turntable hands files over the instant they are picked; the model file is staged into local state and requires a second, explicit press.**

Also `multiple` on one (:349) and not the other (:408-418).

The component branches only on state, never on `what` — `what` is interpolated into sentences (MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx:332, 362, 230). Prototype targets are real: `lengthCm`/`widthCm`/`heightCm`/`diameterCm` all `unit="cm"` (stage_definitions.py:2770-2773).

### Traps and risks

- `e2e/sketches-persistence-unit.spec.ts` reads `PrototypeModelField.tsx` as a raw string (lines 46-48) and asserts literal source substrings. Moving either uploader into a shared component, or renaming the turntable DropCard label, reds it. The exact pins: `"<DropCard"` (:274), ``label={`Add photographs to “${turntableLabel}”`}`` (:275-277), `"multiple"` (:278), `"onFiles={(files) => chooseFrames(files)}"` (:279-281), `"const handed = await onAttachModel(chosen);"` (:250), `"const handed = await onAttachTurntable(frames);"` (:251), and exactly two matches of `/if \(handed === false\) return;/g` (:252). That last count is the trap — merging the two cards into one shared component collapses two occurrences to one.
- The same spec (:290-306) strips comments from `PrototypeModelField.tsx` and then fails on ANY literal DOM `id=` in the remaining markup. Both pickers are `DropCard`s specifically so each derives its ids from its own `useId`; the file records the exact defect this replaced — a hardcoded `id="prototype-model-file"` under a mismatched `htmlFor` (PrototypeModelField.tsx:145-157, 391-407). A unified card must keep one `useId` per instance.
- The turntable card's two paragraphs are quoted into Android as constants whose KDoc claims they are the web's wording VERBATIM (SketchesAndPrototypesScreen.kt:696-697 and :717). Android's own test pins only the FACT clauses — "1 document attached", the download promise, "photograph the piece as well" (DwSketchChooserSentenceTest.kt:267-281) — so a web wording change would break the documented verbatim relationship WITHOUT reddening any test on either client. Copy edits here are a two-client edit.
- `MeasureFromPhotoCard` is a single component shared by both sections (UploadTabHost.tsx:1116-1165). Any restyling done "for prototypes" — radius, background, subtitle width, title weight — lands on the Sketches section too. Its own comment (MeasureFromPhotoCard.tsx:203-207) argues deliberately for `bg-surface-50` over `.panel` so the card does not read as card-on-card inside the section panel; making it match the two `bg-card` prototype cards would reverse a stated decision.
- The two prototype cards' interaction models differ deliberately, not accidentally. The model card stages the file so it can warn BEFORE the transfer (unknown extension, and >64 MB via `LARGE_MODEL_BYTES`, PrototypeModelField.tsx:92-100, 165-186) and so a host refusal does not force a re-pick of a 200 MB file (:200-204). A unified immediate-hand-over uploader deletes both behaviours silently.
- `UploadTabPanel` passes ONE `disabled` for both sections and the host explains why it is `sketchReady || prototypeReady` rather than AND (UploadTabHost.tsx:888-897): a workshop with sketches and no prototypes is the ordinary shape of week one, and ANDing would grey out the live half. Any refactor that pushes readiness per-uploader must keep the per-half refusal sentences (`refuse("prototype")`, :909-920) rather than disabling the control.
- The gap above each section's body is already inconsistent across the two halves and a comment asserts otherwise. Prototypes wraps its field in `mt-3` (UploadTabPanel.tsx:242) while Sketches mounts `SketchTraceField` bare (:220) and that component supplies its own `mt-2` root (SketchTraceField.tsx:2572) — yet the comment at UploadTabPanel.tsx:226-231 states the `mt-3` "matches the gap the Prototypes section puts above its own panel". The comment is true of the measure slots and false of the field slots.
- `formatBytes` is exported from PrototypeModelField.tsx:486 but has no importer anywhere under `components/` or `lib/`. If the file is split during a unification, that export must move with the card that uses it (:181, :440) or be deleted — it is not a shared utility today despite being public.
- Both prototype fields are OPTIONAL in the registry (`A` phase marker, stage_definitions.py:2792-2794) and the turntable is the ONLY field on this whole surface that reaches the printed report as pictures — `report_builder._images` filters on IMAGE and IMAGE_LIST, everything else prints as "1 document attached" (quoted at PrototypeModelField.tsx:21-39). The card's ordering (turntable first) and most of its copy exist to say so. Any layout unification that flattens the two cards into visual equals removes the one signal that they are not equal.

---

## 9. Android sketches / trace / measure — requirements 5, 6, 7, 8, 18, 20 on the handset

**Summary.** Android already ships every capability the web has here, but it ships them in TWO disconnected places and neither of them is a single unified upload. The "Sketches & prototypes" chooser screen has the web's shape — one workshop picker, then an Upload tab and a Review tab — and its Upload tab draws four plain media-capture cards (Sketch image, Line art / vector file, 360° capture, 3D model) and nothing else: no trace panel, no straighten panel, no measure panel, no format list. All three derivation surfaces — "Trace a photographed sketch into line art", "Straighten a photographed sketch into a plate", "Measure a dimension from a photograph" — exist and are fully built, but they are mounted only from the stage form's FieldRenderer, one tap deeper, and they attach to DIFFERENT fields of the same record (measure on the IMAGE field, trace and straighten on the FILE field). None of the three takes an upload of its own; all three read photographs already attached to the record, which is the opposite of the web, whose trace panel carries its own "Photograph to trace" DropCard. The downloadable format list exists as five rows with descriptions but renders as a chip row showing one description at a time, and it is reachable only after a trace has actually run inside the stage form. There is no strings.xml: strings.xml holds exactly one entry (app_name) and there are zero stringResource() calls in the whole app, so every word on screen is a Kotlin literal living beside the Composable that draws it.

### What exists today

- The chooser screen exists and has the web's shape: heading "Sketches & prototypes", a one-line subtitle, ONE workshop picker, then a two-tab strip. Composable is SketchesAndPrototypesScreen(repository, onOpenStage, onError).

- The workshop picker is a SearchableSelectField labelled "Which design workshop", includeNone=false, with four placeholder strings by state: "Looking for your workshops…" / "This list could not be loaded" / "No workshops are listed for this account" / "Choose one of your design workshops".

### Traps and risks

- A 'unified single upload' that files one photograph and then writes a derived artefact back into the SAME field will destroy the original. This is stated twice as a hard rule: 'attaching to a single-valued IMAGE field REPLACES its value, so a panel that wrote a plate onto `image` would detach the original photograph, which docs/MEDIA_PIPELINE.md §5 refuses' (F:/Portal_Development_Designer/android/app/src/main/java/com/designprototype/workshop/ui/designworkshop/FieldRenderer.kt:1882-1886) and again at :1955-1957. It is exactly why the panels are on `lineArtFile` and not on `image`, and why dwOffersSketchTrace delegates rather than re-deriving.
- Any new write on the chooser's Upload tab must respect the reconciled gate. The capture cards are `enabled = half.reconciled` (F:/Portal_Development_Designer/android/app/src/main/java/com/designprototype/workshop/ui/designworkshop/DwSketchChooserUpload.kt:721), meaning the repository's copy of the stage was read and folded in on this pass. Writing a row back over a collection this device has not read is documented as how a stage loses rows nobody deleted (DW_SKETCH_CHOOSER_STALE, DwSketchChooserUpload.kt:840).
- The slotKey convention is load-bearing and easy to break. Cards on the chooser use `"${half.entityKey}:$chosenRow:${field.key}"` (DwSketchChooserUpload.kt:717) and on the stage form `"${field.key}#$resetKey"` (FieldRenderer.kt:1823). Keying on the field alone gives every row in the collection one screening queue and reports row 2's refused photograph under row 7 — stated at both sites.
- The two chooser tab panels are mounted EXCLUSIVELY and the whole subtree is `key(chosen)`-wrapped. Both are deliberate: the review panel coalesces its arrangement push and flushes on unmount, so keeping it composed changes when a reorder reaches the repository, and a picker change mid-window would write workshop A's ordering into workshop B (SketchesAndPrototypesScreen.kt:468-499, DwSketchChooserTabs.kt:85-91). Do not turn the strip into a pager or hoist the panels.
- Single-vs-list media shape is read off the registry, never off the count: `asList = DwFieldType.of(field.type) == DwFieldType.IMAGE_LIST` (DwSketchChooserUpload.kt:404). A one-element array in a single-file field and a bare string in an IMAGE_LIST are both refused by the backend's coerce_value — silently on device, visibly in a ministry document a fortnight later.
- The failure/empty distinction is pinned by a test and is the screen's stated reason for existing. Only DW_SKETCH_CHOOSER_NO_WORKSHOPS may send a designer to an administrator; DwSketchChooserSentenceTest asserts that property and that the retired justification is gone (SketchesAndPrototypesScreen.kt:504-518, :546-577). Any new state added to the Upload tab needs its own sentence rather than reuse of one of these.
- Wording for the prototype 3D fields is the web's verbatim, on purpose, and is argued from three backend files. DW_PROTOTYPE_3D_IN_THE_REPORT and DW_TURNTABLE_CAPTURE_ADVICE (SketchesAndPrototypesScreen.kt:707, :730) are ported word for word from frontend/components/sketches/upload/PrototypeModelField.tsx with exactly one substitution ('rather than in the app' for 'rather than in the browser'). Re-phrasing them is how the second version comes to be slightly wrong about a document a ministry receives.
- The five-format count is derived and must never be typed. DW_TRACE_EXPORT_FORMAT_COUNT (DwSketchTraceExport.kt:489) is read into the on-screen sentence at DwSketchTraceExportCard.kt:243; its KDoc records that the web's equivalent total 'has been mis-stated three different ways in prose'.
- The engine modules are vendored verbatim with a SHA-256 each in F:/Portal_Development_Designer/android/UPSTREAM-MANIFEST-KOTLIN.txt and held to the TypeScript by :core-pipeline's ParityTest against docs/fixtures/. Editing anything under com.offlinetracer.* breaks the manifest and the parity assertion; all app-side adaptation belongs in the DwTraceKotlin* wrappers.
- Android ships far more expensively than the web. Per the project's own reconciliation record, a handset change needs a `v*` tag, a publish and the fleet to come back into signal, and the app runs offline for a fortnight — so anything the handset must understand has to be wire-backward-compatible. Prefer closing this on the web where the capability already exists there.
- Two Android capabilities would be LOST if 'parity' is read as 'make Android match the web': PDF/EPS/DXF export (Android-only, docs/SKETCHES-PROTOTYPES-PARITY.md Matrix B) and adding a NEW sketch or prototype row from the chooser (Android-only deliberate, Matrix D). The web's EXPORT_FORMATS table offers only svg and png.

---

## 10. Requirement 14 — "Experience in Years" for Artisan and Designer becoming two dropdowns (Years + Months) on one line

**Summary.** There are TWO independent experience fields, not one, and they behave differently. `Artisan.experienceYears` is already a DERIVED-first field: since migration 20260823093000 the real answer is `Artisan.craftStartDate` (a date), and `derive_experience_years` computes whole years from it on every read, falling back to the stated `experienceYears Int?` column and then to legacy `extraMetadata` spellings — a three-step precedence implemented identically in the backend, the web client and the Android client. `DesignerProfile.experienceYears Int?` is the opposite: a plain stated integer bounded 0–70 with NO date feeder anywhere on the model (I checked every column: only `empanelmentDate`, which is a different fact). Today both are free-typed NUMBER inputs — `<input type="number">` on web, `OutlinedTextField` with a digits filter on Android — never dropdowns, and there is no months storage, no months field, and no months concept anywhere in the repo (verified by grep across backend/app, backend/prisma, frontend/lib, frontend/components, android/app/src/main: zero hits for `experienceMonths`/`experience_months`). The value fans out through one shared `RecordSpec` into four export surfaces (data-browser info card, /data/report workbook, dataset-zip details.txt, /export CSVs) and through the workshop stage registry into the submitted .docx report, where `participant.experienceYears` is a TABLE_COLUMN whose six sibling column widths already sum to exactly 100. The bounds (0–90 artisan, 0–70 designer) are deliberately mirrored across three-to-four places each and are pinned by guard tests.

### What exists today

- Prisma model Artisan carries the stated integer: `experienceYears Int?` — with a long column comment saying the number ages with the record, not the artisan ("an artisan documented in 2024 with 30 years reads 30 in 2030")
  - `backend/prisma/schema.prisma:652`
- Prisma model Artisan also carries the DATE feeder the years are derived from: `craftStartDate DateTime?`, added 2026-08-23 at the owner's request "experience should also be a derived field, with a field to enter the date of joining the craft serving as the feeder." NULL on every pre-existing row, deliberately
  - `backend/prisma/schema.prisma:628`
- Prisma model DesignerProfile (declared at :3946) carries a SECOND, unrelated `experienceYears Int?`. I enumerated every column on this model: there is NO practice-start date. The only DateTime fields are `empanelmentDate`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt`
  - `backend/prisma/schema.prisma:3958`
- There is no SQLModel in this repo — persistence is Prisma (prisma-client-py) with Pydantic request schemas. Artisan create contract: `craftStartDate: datetime | None = None` (deliberately unbounded) and `experienceYears: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=0, le=90)`
  - `backend/app/schemas/records.py:147`
  - also: backend/app/schemas/records.py:156
- ArtisanUpdate repeats both: `craftStartDate: datetime | None = None` and `experienceYears: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=0, le=90)`
  - `backend/app/schemas/records.py:219`
  - also: backend/app/schemas/records.py:220
- Designer contract, a DIFFERENT ceiling: `experienceYears: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=0, le=70)` on DesignerProfileUpdate (class at :112), bounded to match the registry's `designerExperience` field it prefills
  - `backend/app/schemas/designers.py:138`
- Migration that created the artisan integer plus a backfill from three legacy JSON spellings: `ALTER TABLE "Artisan" ADD COLUMN "experienceYears" INTEGER;` then UPDATE from `extraMetadata ->> 'experienceYears' | 'experience' | 'yearsOfExperience'`, clamped LEAST(GREATEST(x,0),99), only for `~ '^[0-9]{1,3}$'`
  - `backend/prisma/migrations/20260816170000_artisan_dob_and_experience/migration.sql:14`
- Migration that created the date feeder, one nullable additive column with an explicit REFUSAL to backfill: `ALTER TABLE "Artisan" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "craftStartDate" TIMESTAMP(3);`. The file states there is no down-migration in this project and that rollback is a single DROP COLUMN
  - `backend/prisma/migrations/20260823093000_artisan_craft_start_date/migration.sql:105`
- Server derivation, band 0..90, returns None (not 0) outside the band so an out-of-range value is dropped rather than refused: `def derive_experience_years(craft_start: Any, *, on: datetime | None = None) -> int | None` … `return years if 0 <= years <= 90 else None`
  - `backend/app/services/records.py:167`
- Web port of the same derivation: `export function deriveExperienceYears(craftStartDate: string | null | undefined, on?: Date): number | null` returning null outside 0..90
  - `frontend/lib/recordDerivations.ts:133`
- Android port of the same derivation: `fun deriveExperienceYears(craftStartDate: LocalDate?, on: LocalDate = LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC)): Int? = wholeYearsBetween(craftStartDate, on, 0..90)`
  - `android/app/src/main/java/com/designprototype/workshop/ui/DateTimeFields.kt:206`
- WEB ARTISAN FORM — the stated-number input. Exact label text: `Experience (years)` on `<label className="field-label" htmlFor={experienceId}>`, with `<TextInput id={experienceId} name="experienceYears" type="number" inputMode="numeric" min={0} max={90} step={1} defaultValue={initial?.experienceYears ?? ""} …/>`

### Gaps — what the requirement asks for that is not there

- **NO MONTHS STORAGE EXISTS ANYWHERE. There is no `experienceMonths`, `experience_months`, or any months-bearing column, schema field, wire key or UI control in the entire repo.**
  - evidence: Grep for `experienceMonths|experience_months|months.*experience` (case-insensitive) across backend/app, backend/prisma, frontend/lib, frontend/components and android/app/src/main returned zero matches. The only experience storage is `Artisan.experienceYears Int?` (backend/prisma/schema.prisma:652), `Artisan.craftStartDate DateTime?` (backend/prisma/schema.prisma:628) and `DesignerProfile.experienceYears Int?` (backend/prisma/schema.prisma:3958).
  - change here: backend/prisma/schema.prisma (Artisan block ~:628-652 and DesignerProfile block :3946-3958); a new additive migration directory under backend/prisma/migrations/
  - android twin: NONE — android/app/src/main/java/com/designprototype/workshop/data/ApiModels.kt has only `experienceYears`/`craftStartDate` (:746, :753, :1193, :1194, :3097, :3152)
- **NEITHER FIELD IS A DROPDOWN TODAY. All four input sites are free-typed numeric text boxes, and the requirement asks for two selects on one line.**
  - evidence: Web artisan: `<TextInput … type="number" inputMode="numeric" min={0} max={90} step={1}>` (frontend/components/forms/ArtisanForm.tsx:1355). Web designer: `<TextInput name="experienceYears" type="number" min={0} max={70} step={1}>` (frontend/components/designers/DesignerProfileForm.tsx:462). Android artisan: `OutlinedTextField(… keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number)` (android/…/MainActivity.kt:7966). Android designer: `OutlinedTextField(… label = { Text("Years of experience") } …)` (android/…/DesignerProfileScreen.kt:986).
  - change here: frontend/components/forms/ArtisanForm.tsx:1329-1376 (replace the single label+TextInput+hint block with a two-column grid of Selects); frontend/components/designers/DesignerProfileForm.tsx:461-473; android/…/MainActivity.kt:7966-7991; android/…/DesignerProfileScreen.kt:986-1004
  - android twin: android/app/src/main/java/com/designprototype/workshop/MainActivity.kt:7966 and android/app/src/main/java/com/designprototype/workshop/ui/designworkshop/DesignerProfileScreen.kt:986
- **THE ARTISAN FIELD IS ALREADY DERIVED FROM A DATE, so a Years+Months dropdown pair is a THIRD statement of the same fact competing with `craftStartDate`. The requirement does not say what wins.**
  - evidence: backend/app/services/design_workshops.py:1549 puts `derive_experience_years(r.craftStartDate)` FIRST in the precedence, ahead of the stated column; backend/app/services/record_fields.py:456-460 does the same for every record-sheet surface; the migration comment at backend/prisma/migrations/20260823093000_artisan_craft_start_date/migration.sql:14-19 records this as the owner's explicit request.
  - change here: backend/app/services/records.py:167 (needs a sibling `derive_experience_months` on the same date, band 0..11); backend/app/services/design_workshops.py:1549; backend/app/services/record_fields.py:443; plus the two client ports frontend/lib/recordDerivations.ts:133 and android/…/DateTimeFields.kt:206
  - android twin: android/app/src/main/java/com/designprototype/workshop/ui/DateTimeFields.kt:206
- **THE DESIGNER FIELD HAS NO DATE FEEDER AT ALL, so months cannot be derived there under any scheme — it must be stored.**
  - evidence: I enumerated every column of `model DesignerProfile` (backend/prisma/schema.prisma:3946-4010): id, userId, displayName, localName, designation, institution, department, qualification, specialisation, experienceYears, biography, phone, email, website, addressLine, city, state, pincode, photoMediaId, signatureMediaId, cvMediaId, empanelmentNo, empanelmentDate, createdAt, updatedAt. The only practice-adjacent DateTime is `empanelmentDate`, which is empanelment, not craft practice.
  - change here: backend/prisma/schema.prisma:3958 (add `experienceMonths Int?`); backend/app/schemas/designers.py:138; backend/app/services/designers.py:39 (PROFILE_FIELDS) and :228 (PREFILL_MAP); frontend/lib/designers.ts:313 and :449; frontend/components/designers/profileCopy.ts:46/:110/:179; frontend/components/designers/DesignerProfileView.tsx:115
  - android twin: android/app/src/main/java/com/designprototype/workshop/data/ApiModels.kt:3097 and :3152, plus the hand-written encoder at :3204
- **THE REPORT REGISTRY CANNOT ABSORB A MONTHS TABLE_COLUMN without re-laying out an already-submitted participant table.**
  - evidence: backend/app/services/stage_definitions.py:816-834 states the six TABLE_COLUMN widths on the participant entity "already sum to exactly 100", that `_table_columns` takes `columns[:6]` in declaration order, and that a seventh is either sliced off and printed nowhere or pushes the sixth out and breaks the 100±0.5 sum. `participant.experienceYears` is `report_role=COL, column_width_pct=12.0` (:805-806).
  - change here: backend/app/services/stage_definitions.py:805-852 — a months field must be declared `report_role=KV` (the way `craftStartDate` at :848 was), or the six existing widths must all be re-proportioned
  - android twin: NONE — the registry is server-side; clients receive it over the wire via stage_schema.field_to_dict (backend/app/services/stage_schema.py:2512)
- **THE DESIGNER LABEL IS ALREADY INCONSISTENT ACROSS CLIENTS, and the requirement's rewording will fork it further if not fixed in the same change.**
  - evidence: Web renders `Designer’s experience` (frontend/components/designers/profileCopy.ts:46, sourced verbatim from the registry field at backend/app/services/stage_definitions.py:363). Android renders `Years of experience` (android/…/DesignerProfileScreen.kt:995) and its error text says "Years of experience must be a whole number between 0 and 70." (:680).
  - change here: frontend/components/designers/profileCopy.ts:46; android/app/src/main/java/com/designprototype/workshop/ui/designworkshop/DesignerProfileScreen.kt:995 and :680; backend/app/services/stage_definitions.py:363
  - android twin: android/app/src/main/java/com/designprototype/workshop/ui/designworkshop/DesignerProfileScreen.kt:995
- **FOUR EXPORT SURFACES WOULD SILENTLY DISAGREE if the storage shape changes without touching the one shared spec.**
  - evidence: backend/app/services/record_fields.py:443-461 is a single `_f("Experience (years)", …)` entry whose label is the literal export column header; `sheet_columns`/`sheet_row` (:663, :674) feed backend/app/api/routes/datasets.py:102 (dataset-zip .xlsx) and backend/app/services/csv_export.py:28 (CSV downloads), while `info_panel`/`info_text` (:641, :654) feed backend/app/api/routes/export.py:27 and backend/app/api/routes/data_browser.py:110. The docstring at :445-451 names all four and warns they "would otherwise disagree with the participant table about one artisan's experience".
  - change here: backend/app/services/record_fields.py:443 only — all four surfaces are generic over the spec and none of them mention experience by name (verified: grep for `experience` in data_browser.py, csv_export.py, datasets.py and export.py returns nothing)
  - android twin: NONE — exports are server-only
- **NO WEB READ-ONLY ARTISAN RECORD VIEW EXISTS to update; the artisan read surface is server-rendered.**

There is no frontend/components/records or frontend/components/artisans directory. The read-only display comes from backend/app/services/record_fields.py:443 via the data browser info card.
